- Node: v24.16.0
- NODE_ENV: production
- pnpm: 10.19.0
- Platform: linux x64
- CPU: AMD EPYC 9V74 80-Core Processor (4)
- Memory: 16766423040 bytes

  - svelte: 5.56.3
  - vue: 3.5.38

## Rankings

### app render 1000 nodes

| ---: | --- | --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
